# M6 consolidated record, volume D3: CP3 P13 execution history from 2026-11-09

Historical reference only. These 71 superseded execution records and one closed continuation note are not active instructions. The two navigation preimages preserve displaced context without replaying old turn ladders. Every original is available at `6ef77580617731bc2d5e364b924c706ad9bb0b34` (`git show 6ef77580617731bc2d5e364b924c706ad9bb0b34:.agents/Directional/<filename>`). Original SHA-256 and line counts are indexed below. Full record content is retained; presentation only: trim trailing whitespace and fence the original Markdown to prevent historical commands and turn headings from becoming live instructions. No criterion, outcome, source or evidence identity is omitted. Current review, CB144, TB125/TB126, normative definitions, exact frozen diffs and selectors remain separate. See [current handoff](Future_Chat_Session_Handoff.md) and [moved-record index](M6_Consolidated_Record.md#tb126-review-folded-record-index-2026-11-17).

# TB109 — annulus rim-edge schedule full-gate execution (2026-11-12 UTC)

## Situation

**COMPLETE / scoped MATCH.** The immutable CB128 package completed the 944-case gate with **944/944 PASS** and **1,025** memberships. Its three-case production census had Cylinder **PASS** and BunnyRandom/Vase **registered RED**, unchanged from TB108. This is test/benchmark evidence only, not promotion of CP3 or selector497. CB129 is **not authorized** because the row-729 equal-output condition fails.

## Background

- **Canonical turn:** `M6-CP3-TB109-P13-A3-ANNULUS-RIM-EDGE-SCHEDULE-FULL-GATE-EXEC` (64D.5), artifact-only; no source, test, fixture, selector, harness, compilation, relink, configuration or benchmark changes.
- **Immutable CB128 source:** `739e79e240f75ac1c24e659004b13b425803081b`.
- **Compiled package:** [run 43150206247](https://github.com/akashskypatel/Directional/actions/runs/43150206247), artifact `13913578057`, ZIP SHA-256 `1eb3f762034dff9cf31f1ba45362a9f1b371eda35986fc062f3bd521e679f394`, 28/28 package checksums and eight GMP/GMPXX targets built; compile record confirms `runtimeExecution=false`.
- **Full-gate manifest:** `.agents/Directional/gates/Architecture_M6_TB109_P13_A3_Annulus_Rim_Edge_Schedule_Full_Gate_Case_Manifest.tsv`, SHA-256 `46e5c8a8ffb1a8d6337d782bef44fa852d3b17870e2f06ab037d55a8ea201f61`, 944 rows, 1,025 memberships, deferred ledger 81.
- **Full gate:** [run 43155345821](https://github.com/akashskypatel/Directional/actions/runs/43155345821), event `85478405d12b5c9fadfe92c504b3ccbbb7bcce40`, artifact `13916127330`, ZIP SHA-256 `b5757bf58343f31d47df024405ce92510323eafccecc16ac32ec83c7df55885d`.
- **Production census:** [run 43155374160](https://github.com/akashskypatel/Directional/actions/runs/43155374160), event `27513cf95e6d93f850b7f1eefe0a00c245ba0521`, artifact `13915439007`, ZIP SHA-256 `4147c5e2afa15d8deb08dfa26abaf3b47b7e5fc1a2648c1519be309e141ba1d0`.
- **Prior comparison:** TB108 gate `13908358480` (ZIP SHA `ac6a9232e0924e2cb973c11c5c96cb679cf0f7c37a404dc7034f7b4b7a1f6bd1`) and census `13908514778` (ZIP SHA `a136ff56e27bf08297cf9b6dc7ba6c1ca1ba7d1b3568b838428a43c3a8d1dece`).

## Assessment — independent artifact validation

| Control | Verified result |
|---|---|
| Full case ledger | 944 planned / 944 executed / **944 PASS**; zero RED, skipped, abnormal, missing, duplicates, unexpected PASS, control failure, or later-positive RED |
| Membership | 1,025; row 944 is the new annulus rim reader test |
| Full-gate SHA256SUMS | **1,896/1,896** entries valid |
| Inherited case records | First 943 substantive ledger rows match TB108, excluding per-process log digests |
| Full-gate inherited receipts | 19/19 unchanged, plus 4/4 `rim-branch` and 12/12 `annulus-rim` lines unchanged |
| Census result | Cylinder PASS; BunnyRandom and Vase registered RED; no unexpected changes |
| Census SHA256SUMS | **9/9** entries valid |
| Census inherited receipts | Six lines unchanged: three `platform-digest`, three `a3-coverage`; all substantive ledger rows unchanged |
| Total receipt equivalence | **41/41** unchanged (19 gate + 6 census + 4 rim-branch + 12 annulus-rim) |
| Execution boundary | Both report exact CB128 source/package, postflight true, exit 0, and `compile=false`, `configure=false`, `relink=false`, `benchmark=false` |

The new row-729 receipt is **verbatim**:

```text
annulus-read case=CommittedCylinder rimEdges=32 readable=32 equalOutput=24 outputRimEdges=56 recorded=0
```

RA-228 §5 requires `recorded=0` and `outputRimEdges>0`, both met. Its **conditional permission for CB129 fails**: `readable=32`, but `equalOutput=24` differs from `rimEdges=32`. This is a pairing/count-consistency design issue to resolve in a separate Review. This result is not a reason to change the existing test oracle or claim production acceptance.

The existing production failures are unchanged: BunnyRandom stops with `InvalidFieldTransportAtlas:field-transport-atlas/SingularityPortClassCountMismatch`, and Vase with `NotProductionReady:completion` after 189,462 traces, 26,817 arrangement cells and 67,750 partial completion quads. Their two RED verdicts are already registered as non-stable candidate limitations and do not add a new stable regression event.

Stable accepted CP2 remains **491/491**; regression accounting remains **66 events / 17 categories / 49 recurrences**, debt **1**, deferred **81**. O36 remains unwired. CP3/selector497 remain unpromoted; PR #8 remains draft/open/unmerged.

## Recommendation

Close TB109 as **COMPLETE / scoped MATCH**, with the *next separate Review-only* `M6-CP3-DEFN-P13-A3-ANNULUS-RIM-PAIRING` (RA-228 §5). It must determine how A3 maintains equal paired rim counts at row 729 before any CB129 implementation. The implementation agent must **not** apply the frozen CB129 patch, begin TB110, modify O36, or promote CP3/selector497 in this turn.

Verification was performed from downloaded raw ZIP artifacts and an independent read-only Python checker. The previous session's repository read-mode sequencing miss was a procedural issue and did not change the immutable runtime evidence. No fresh build was run.

**Scoped MATCH** satisfies all RA-231 §5 conditions. There is **no new stable regression**. ## Assessment — exact gate failure

| Gate item | Observed |
|---|---|
| 949 registered cases | 949 executed, 0 skipped, 0 missing, 0 duplicate, 0 abnormal |
| 949/949 PASS requirement | **FAIL: 948 PASS, 1 RED** |
| Membership and deferred | **1,030** and **81**, as frozen |

Row 949 fails in its `cross` subcase. The test expects `elected.nextBranch == follow.continuation.nextBranch`, but the exact log records `elected.nextBranch = 2`, `follow.continuation.nextBranch = 3`. The test's own expected continuation *face* and *kind* were not reported as failures. The source code assigns the continuation branch from the destination sector, not by copying the elected branch. Branch values belong to source-face branch frames; cross-face numeric equality is not established by this log. **The defect's owner is not yet proven.** Independent Review must check the test's asserted branch relation against the actual field-transport contract and inspect whether the producer selects a correct branch. Do **not** weaken or remove the assertion merely to obtain a green result, and do not label the source production algorithm correct on this evidence alone.

No gate log contains such a line.

## Regression classification and recommendation

1. **New required-green control failure, non-stable candidate.** Row 949 is the only lost required gate row. The failing assertion compares local branch numbers across two faces after a `Cross` transition. Whether the source or the oracle is wrong requires Review. ## RA-238 COMPLETE (P13-e.9; obtuse-face edge port + exact slide frozen) — next: `M6-CP3-CB138-P13-OBTUSE-EDGE-PORT-SLIDE` (implementation agent, compile only) — 2026-11-16 UTC

**Canonical completed turn:** `M6-CP3-DEFN-P13-EDGE-PORT-TRACE-OUTCOME` (Review only). [RA-238](Architecture_M6_CP3_DEFN_P13_Edge_Port_Trace_Outcome_Review_20261116.md).
- **TB118 accepted** as a scoped MATCH. The duplicated legacy `M3_CP4AB_FRAGMENT_DIAG` text is pre-existing nondeterminism, so later MATCH conditions compare receipt lines, not whole raw logs.
- **Vertex 367 root cause.** A1's attach test (weight on S < 0 in both faces) stands in for the slide direction and fails when the face is obtuse at S. In face (162,170,367), vertex 162 projects at r = −1.23 behind 367, so w₃₆₇ = +8.77 although the exact rate along the edge, away from 367, is positive in both faces (0.005438 and 0.005415). A model on the repository mesh and the TB118 direction lines reproduces all 11 logged `attached` bits.
- **A2a has the same flaw.** The grazing transit's endpoint-rate test on `carrier.second()` = 367 is undecided there, so CB137's departure would stop with `BranchGrazingSlideDirectionAmbiguous`.
- **Fix (CB138).**
  - A1 attaches iff T = d·(x_far − x_S) > 0 in both faces. This is exact on the stored doubles and independent of vertex numbering.
  - A2a's edge-port departure slides to the carrier's far vertex through the new `resolve_field_edge_port_slide`; ordinary grazing is unchanged.
  - New test `FieldTransportAtlas.PublishesEdgeSupportedPortFromFaceObtuseAtVertex` uses a planar fan with a 140° face, the same shape as vertex 367. It fails on CB137 with `SingularityPortClassCountMismatch`.
- **Gate neutral.** Rows 1–945 have no straddle (TB118), and row 946 still attaches (T = 1.190 and 1.190).
- **Frozen:**
  - [CB138 exact patch](Architecture_M6_CP3_CB138_P13_Obtuse_Edge_Port_Slide_Exact.diff) `53985fef66e2e7262d4624c79f5b037e58e4760f4db18403d37c683a4e0e5d60` (334 lines, keep 5 single-space context lines; 4 files, `+211 −25`).

## Purpose — DURABLE, DO NOT DELETE

This file contains durable operating policy, the exact next action, current immutable authority, standing product state, and resume-critical information required to continue safely. Architecture belongs in `DESIGN.md`; tasks in `TODO.md`; completed history in `CHANGELOG.md`; lessons in `LESSONS.md`; roadmap in `ROADMAP.md`; exact runtime evidence in retained reports/closure records and the regression tracker. Do not add transcripts, chronological tool history, tool/policy/procedural failures, copied old artifact tables, obsolete selections, or procedure owned by policy/skill files. Concision never authorizes deleting durable information.

This section is durable and must be preserved. The displaced chronological handoff remains in Git history, **not** as instructions to replay.

All instructions and sections prefixed `[ChatGPT Web]` are mandatory for `ChatGPT Web` sessions. Non-`ChatGPT Web` agents (`ChatGPT Work`, `Codex`, `Claude`, etc.) can ignore instructions/sections bearing that prefix.

### Step 64D.3 completed (RA-228)

[RA-228](Architecture_M6_CP3_DEFN_P13_A3_Annulus_Rim_Schedule_Review_20261112.md) freezes the annulus rim reader.
- Both producers read an arc on its source edge through one function. The bounded-disk producer keeps its cursor join, which allows bridging; the annulus does not.
- An annulus strip is readable when both rim edges split exactly into `k/n` with the same `n` and every arc end on a breakpoint. Keeping the split exact leaves today's coordinates bit-identical wherever `n` is unchanged.
- Under the per-arc schedule, Cylinder reads on every rim edge (TB108). Its chained 28/32 comes from two two-arc chains whose lengths differ by a rounding unit.
- O36 clause: annulus rim vertices are chain stops.
- Row 729 runs the Cylinder fixture at a length ratio no census covered, so TB109 measures it before CB129 switches.

### Step 64D completed (RA-227)

[RA-227](Architecture_M6_CP3_DEFN_P13_A3_Annulus_Plan_Consumption_Review_20261112.md) settles D4 for the annulus. The periodic annulus producer does not read A3: it counts each strip from its own length and records no interval. It cannot decline the way the uniform producer does, because no other producer builds an Euler-0 region. So under a plan it consumes its two rims. A strip is readable when both of its rim edges have breakpoints at their ends and the same split. Under the per-arc schedule that is today's placement, `k/n`. A chained schedule spreads ordinals by run weight, so it usually bridges a rim vertex, and the annulus's product lattice cannot read that. The ring scoring (RA-226 F4) gets no code: its score is a sum of squared `acos` values, it is not an A3 reader, and exact ties already fail closed. The reader itself is frozen by the Review-only follow-on after TB108, because its digest set and the O36 clause depend on TB108's numbers.

### Step 64B.23 completed (RA-226)

[RA-226](Architecture_M6_CP3_DEFN_P13_A3_Exact_Rim_Arithmetic_Review_20261111.md) defines the exact rim branch rule (O39). The inputs are the stored doubles of the face's vertices and axes, read as exact rationals. With the unnormalized normal `N` and the in-plane inner product `g(p,q) = (p·q)(N·N) − (p·N)(q·N)`, a family's `cos²` with the edge is `g(A,E)² / (g(A,A) g(E,E))`. The family with the larger `cos²` wins, equal squares tie, and the winner needs `cos ≥ 7/10`. There is no tolerance and no normalization. The binary64 rule it replaces depends on scale and on an axis's normal component, and decides near-ties by rounding. Two scope items get no code: the fan carry keeps the atlas quarter-turn and the tracer's forward test, whose operand has a 45° margin under principal matching; and no vertex-level Changed/Undecided type is added, because both are stops and nothing reads the difference. TB104 and TB105 matched RA-225's predictions.

### Step 64B.18 completed (RA-225)

[RA-225](Architecture_M6_CP3_DEFN_P13_A3_Boundary_Fan_Stop_Identity_Review_20261111.md) answers the fan-identity question the TB101C reviews raised. The network has one node per source vertex, and a chain joins only at a node with exactly two incidences. A vertex shared by two fans is a node with four, so no chain passes it and a vertex-keyed stop cannot cut another fan's chain. The resolver, the per-incidence join pairs and the fan-scoped filter are withdrawn. The reviews' other two points are kept: a derived-corner witness (CB123) and one owner for the branch rule (CB124), with the exact rule (O39) as a Review definition before the switch. RA-225 also records that row 938 of TB100 failed on a wrong expected value in RA-224: the atlas has no transport across a hard-feature edge.

## Authoritative adversarial review and narrow turn order — 2026-11-11

[TB100 review audit](Architecture_M6_CP3_TB100_REV_Audit_20261111.md) independently checked the 938-row gate and the C1 source/atlas path. The earlier test-oracle classification stands **only** for the hard-rail fixture. The source-boundary product is unwired; the current gate does not accept production. O39 exact-topology debt is a mandatory pre-O36 condition under DESIGN Amendment 18, not optional on platform mismatch. Old hard-rail 8-corner prediction is superseded by 12; the four endpoint classifications are source inputs, not output-derived expectations.

**Technical re-review correction (owner request, 2026-11-11):** The 938/938 gate is valid only for its exact test scope; the previous Review had two false stop-set falsifiers. Existing hard-feature endpoints already stop, including off-rim ones; a pass-through rim field corner **must** stop. ### Step 64.5 re-entered (RA-217)

- **Stands:** the placement formula, the zero-weight reversal analysis, the single-piece `(j,n)` rule, baseline-only ownership with `ConformityScheduleEntry`/`GlobalConformityPlan.cpp:49` frozen, and single-piece-only `denominator == n`.

- **G3:** floor weights of zero would fail the baseline build. Now ceiling weights `⌈2⁶⁴·metric⌉ ≥ 1` from the same exact squared metric; reversal stays exact.
- **G4:** "canonical source entity" and "typed provenance" are not representable; the half-open rule decides joins.
- **G5:** the width receipt keeps M4 §5.5's "values encountered by validation" (ordinals 0, 1, n−1, n); single-piece widths unchanged.
- **G6:** the validator re-derives from the input via the shared function, as for the count.

### Step 64 re-entered (RA-216)

- **Stands:** the root cause (`√(m·Σλ²)` and piece-index placement), the ρ = interpolated 1/h density, collinear canonicalization, tie-down and minimum one, and scope separation.
- **F1:** exact rounding of a sum of square roots is the sum-of-square-roots decision. The kernel decides one root, and the only multi-radical code is exponential in the number of roots. The count is now run-wise ⌊2⁶⁴·metric⌋, rounded once.
- **F2:** exact inverse breakpoints would be irrational, contrary to §7.4's exact source-rail coordinates and every rational consumer identity. D7b places by metric across runs and by length within a run.
- **F3:** curved-disk, the only placement consumer, takes single-piece edge arcs only. The count lands now; placement lands before D5.
- **F4:** the framed CP1/CP2 copies stay frozen.
- **F5:** single-piece counts change wherever h varies, so the digest outcome is pre-registered (TB89 identical; TB90 rule).
- **F6:** CB106 is now exact, and CB107 is added.
- **F7:** where the placement run profile lives is a D7b decision.
- **F8:** the kink and verifier falsifiers are rebound.

## Resume-critical lessons — DURABLE, DO NOT DELETE

The substantive lessons live in `LESSONS.md`; use the relevant section rather than copying incident accounts.

| Section | Read it before |
|---|---|
| 1. Evidence, acceptance, and scope | Interpreting results, claiming acceptance, changing gates/selectors or classifying regressions. |
| 2. Witnesses, tests, and independent oracles | Designing fixtures, tests, invariants or independent checks. |
| 3. Semantic ownership, identity, and dataflow | Modifying semantic IDs, representations or producer-consumer contracts. |
| 4. Geometry, topology, and mathematical obligations | Changing geometric algorithms, topology or exact predicates. |
| 5. Diagnostics, classifiers, and causal investigation | Instrumentation, rejecting predicates or root-cause hypotheses. |
| 6. Contracts, planning, reviews, and durable records | Requirements, plans, reviews, authorization or documentation. |
| 7. Build, artifact, workflow, and tool mechanics | Compilation, packaging, CI, artifacts or repository operations. |

New lessons belong in `LESSONS.md`, not here.

## Maintenance — one live handoff

At authorized closeout, **replace** current authority, next action, conditional successor and context **in place**. Keep the durable purpose/policy/load/lesson sections. Archive completed changes in `CHANGELOG.md`, evidence in reports/tracker, tasks in `TODO.md`. Never append a second `CURRENT` section, a transcript or old turn ladder. The mandatory end checklist and `Durable_Handoff_Policy.md` govern `STATUS` closeout.

  - **Noise carriers:** the atlas digest hashes exact `effort` bits, and five later hashes embed it, so they change with any last-bit field change.
  - **The field is not the cause:** five distinct Vase fields give the same macOS output.
  - **The cause:** `SurfaceArrangement.cpp:6762` assigns cell ids in a non-injective key order. With randomized `std::sort` ties, one build gives Vase 67,753 to 67,827 quads.
  - **The fix:** CB90 adds a halfedge tie-break. After it, Vase gives 67,746 under every order, and the local gate is 898/899 (only O16).
- **RA-205:** RA-204's mechanism and CB90 stand. Completed counts that change with the cell schedule violate DESIGN §10.1. A Vase source-face row permutation changes seeds (1,456 → 1,461), traces, strands and quads (67,746 → 67,771–67,784). That is O32.
- **CB91 and TB81:** [CB91 record](M6_Consolidated_Record.md#m6-folded-document-index-2026-11-09--durable-do-not-delete "folded: Architecture_M6_CP3_CB91_P13_Final_Validation_Binding_Code_Build_Record_20261105.md") on source `96ee281a…`; [TB81](M6_Consolidated_Record_Vol_D2_CP3_P13_from_20261104.md#architecture_m6_cp3_tb81_p13_final_validation_binding_full_gate_execution_report_20261105md "moved: Architecture_M6_CP3_TB81_P13_Final_Validation_Binding_Full_Gate_Execution_Report_20261105.md") 899/899 and seven TB80 digest lines identical. Predecessor TB80 remains a reviewed historical 898/899 timing-control RED; O32a is next, not another TB81 replay.
- Review track (~~Claude, scratch build~~ runtime-free since RA-214 §1; historical list):
  - `M6-CP3-DEFN-P13-ROW-PERMUTATION-SEEDS` (O32a, next);
  - `M6-CP3-DEFN-P13-COMPLETED-MESH-STITCH-OVERLAP` (P13-l2, after TB83; RA-208.6, RA-209.5);
  - `M6-CP3-DEFN-P13-SINGULARITY-PORT-COUNT` (P13-e);
  - `M6-CP3-DEFN-P13-RELIEF-NOISE-TIES` (O31, after P13-l).
**Branch:** `agent/surface_cell_quad/p5-recover-bridge-healing` (experimental, PR #8).
**Purpose:** Help a fresh contributor understand the product, architecture, fixtures, project records, cadence, current status and outlook **in minutes**—without reading a turn-by-turn journal.

**Start here →** this page → [ROADMAP.md](ROADMAP.md) (milestones) → [DESIGN.md](DESIGN.md) §§2, 5, 14 (normative architecture) → [Future_Chat_Session_Handoff.md](Future_Chat_Session_Handoff.md) (exact current authority/next turn). Read the relevant frozen definitions and policies before touching implementation.

## 1. What the project is

Directional is a C++ directional-field library with a Python interface ([README](../../README.md)). This branch develops a **direct surface-cell quadrangulation backend**: given a source triangle mesh, tangent cross field, source features and target size, produce a deterministic, source-attached, manifold **pure-quad mesh** with independently verifiable guarantees.

The chosen architecture is **Pipeline B**: field-aligned combinatorial tracing **without global coordinate integration**. Its central ideas are:

- **Single-writer authority:** one producer owns each semantic fact; downstream stages consume immutable, typed products and never repair earlier results.
- **Exact source and topology:** field transport, incidence, region ownership and quotient identity are certified—not inferred from position, container order, a hash or an epsilon.
- **Global-before-local conformity:** choose shared-rail counts/breakpoints once, before independently constructing regional quads.
- **Typed outcomes and real evidence:** fail with an owning reason; prove the intended stage was reached using independent oracles, positive cases, mutations and invariance tests.
- **Verified disposition:** `D0 Certified` through `D4 DiagnosticOnly` describe the *eventual* independently assigned guarantees. M7 has not yet implemented the full ladder.

The objective is **production-ready Pipeline B at M8-CP3**, not merely passing internal contract tests. An optional coordinate-integration **Pipeline A** is documented but unscheduled.

## 2. Pipeline at a glance

| Stage | Owns |
|---|---|
| **Conditioning → A0** | Certified raw→conditioned correspondence; `SourceAuthoritySnapshot` with exact source support, barriers, components, sheets and typed identities. |
| **A1** | `FieldTransportAtlas`: field frames, transport, singularities and holonomy. |
| **A2a → A2a′ → A2b** | `FieldAlignedCurveNetwork` traces; `SurfaceCutGraph` cellularity; `GlobalTopologyPlan` regions with disc proofs. |
| **A3 → A4** | One `GlobalConformityPlan` for shared rails; conforming `RegionCellComplex` products per region. |
| **A5 → A6 → A7** | Explicit corner/side occurrences; only certified quotient merges; source-attached vertex geometry. |
| **A8 → A9** | Independent verification; eventual M7 verifier-assigned disposition; derived diagnostics and export. |

Each arrow denotes a checked, immutable producer-to-consumer boundary. Details and stop conditions belong to [DESIGN.md](DESIGN.md#5-normative-pipeline--pipeline-b), not this map.

## 4. Fixtures and witnesses

**Current TB126 witnesses:** Cylinder PASS, 288 quads. BunnyRandom RED `RotationSystemInconsistent` / `CellularityNotEstablished`, face `(5,104,359)` (no accepted output); all four inventory records and upstream receipts match TB125, whose earlier failure was edge `(241,251)`. Vase RED at completion unchanged (189,462 segments; 26,817 arrangement cells; 67,750 internal quads; no accepted output). Not full direct-production acceptance.

**A3 exactness and the API display witness:** A3 branch topology (face gauge, barycentric derivatives, port order) is exact on the stored doubles (RA-180/RA-181); FMA and FMA-free builds agree byte for byte. Row 935 (`apicensus:19`) is gated with a +½-singularity vertex stop (RA-182). Exact directions widen A4 trace parameters (O15c; for example sphere_prescribed 1,531 of 4,096 bits, fail-closed), and P13 measures them.

The **ten-case representative direct matrix** is defined by [tests/TESTING_STRATEGY.md §4](../../tests/TESTING_STRATEGY.md#4-mandatory-committed-direct-matrix), using [benchmarks/fixtures/milestone-g/](../../benchmarks/fixtures/milestone-g/) and the [fixture matrix manifest](../../benchmarks/fixtures/milestone_g_manifest.json).

| Representative fixture | Intended coverage |
|---|---|
| `plane`, `cylinder` | Basic open/bounded surfaces and boundary topology. |
| `multi_face_seam` | Cross-face seam and field-transport behavior. |
| `close_sheets` | Near-coincident but distinct source sheets. |
| `torus` | Closed, periodic and noncontractible topology. |
| `thin_bent_tube` | Curvature, thinness and approximation stress. |
| `sphere_prescribed` | Prescribed field, singularities and closed-surface tracing. |
| `mechanical_feature` | Hard-feature and trace/rail preservation. |
| `bunny_1k_random` (Bunny) | Representative mesh with generated smooth field. |
| `vase` | Representative mesh with generated smooth field. |

**Other focused witness:** `genus_two` tests higher-genus behavior but is **not an eleventh case** in the mandatory ten-case direct matrix. Synthetic test-local witnesses prove narrow contracts; they cannot substitute for pipeline-produced representative evidence.

Fixture generation, provenance, licenses and checksums are recorded in [generate_milestone_g_fixtures.py](../../benchmarks/fixtures/generate_milestone_g_fixtures.py) and [asset-manifest.json](../../benchmarks/fixtures/milestone-g/asset-manifest.json). The matrix also contains legacy-backend comparisons; **strict direct** testing selects `SurfaceCells`, `SurfaceCellFallbackPolicy::Fail`, with source-grid recovery disabled. **A present fixture or focused green gate does not establish D0 acceptance across the full matrix.**

## 6. Development and evidence cadence

| Turn | Responsibility and boundary |
|---|---|
| **DEFN / Plan** | Freeze scoped semantics, admissible witnesses, acceptance and owner; **no** compile/runtime. |
| **CB — Code + Build** | Implement authorized changes and compile/package exact source; mandatory **GMP and GMPXX** linkage. Compile success is not runtime acceptance. |
| **TB — Test + Benchmark** | Fresh tests against immutable package with exact selector; **no** rebuild, repair, semantic mutation or invented discovery. Publish PASS **and RED** evidence. |
| **REV — independent Review** | Re-open primary evidence, re-derive decisive facts, test non-vacuity, accept/reject promotion and assign unresolved obligations. Same agent is permitted; independence is of **evidence**, not personnel. |

The separation is deliberate: accepted selector continuity and frozen invariants are not negotiable fixes for a RED; diagnostics are not acceptance; representative production claims require production-path witnesses. See the [review policy](REVIEW_TURN_POLICY.md), [testing strategy](../../tests/TESTING_STRATEGY.md) and [workflow policy](GitHub_Workflow_Policy.md) for actual requirements, repository operation, artifact transport and mailbox behavior.

## 8. Recurring defect patterns

- **Invalid typed-ID witness extent:** before freezing a guard, verify every ID against its own domain extent. A factory error or exception before the producer call does not falsify the producer; preserve the meaningful assertions and correct only the invalid input. See [RA-245](Architecture_M6_CP3_TB126_P13_Edge_Terminal_Correction_Independent_Review_20261117.md).

- **Cross-face identifiers need transport (TB122-REV):** two branch numbers in distinct canonical face frames are not directly comparable, even when raw face matching is zero. Build an independent destination field/wedge oracle instead of comparing face-local branch tags. Also distinguish unsupported multiple cuts from demonstrated incompatible geometry. [Review](Architecture_M6_CP3_TB122_REV_P13_Vertex_Transit_Carrier_Seed_And_Outflow_Independent_Review_20261116.md).

- **Full-set oracle exceeds a scoped equivalence (TB100-REV):** a pass-through-rim equality does not prohibit conservative corners at hard-feature barriers. Independent test expectations must include every C1 admissible cause and derive barrier endpoints from source topology, not copy the product. See [M6-CP3-TB100-P13-A3-BOUNDARY-FIELD-CORNERS-FULL-GATE-REV](Architecture_M6_CP3_TB100_REV_P13_A3_Boundary_Field_Corner_Oracle_Adjudication_20261111.md).

- **A DEFN that keeps adding proof gates is in the wrong layer (RA-218):** the stalled D5 attempt put chains in a separate diagnostic product beside unchanged A3 spans. Every gate it added (fragment sides, corner re-derivation, identity migration, a production join before any CB) followed from that choice. Ask which stage owns the quantity being changed. D5 changes a scheduling count, so the A3 span set changes and A2b does not; degree-2 pass-through nodes make side and region continuity automatic.

- **A source face is not one embedded graph-face fragment (D5 independent-proof review):** one original `SourceFaceTopologyKey` may support several trace-cut A2b fragments/orbits. Source-region membership and sheet identify input provenance, not the ownership of a particular *directed arc side*. Recompute the oriented graph-face boundary occurrence from original A2b arcs/rotation/region walks before proving a join; a hash or same-face equality cannot supply that binding. This is a specification-falsifier refinement, not a measured new runtime regression.

- **A receipt minted before the act it certifies (RA-214 D8):** `SurfaceConformityPlanReceipt` says A4 "consumed" A3, but the aggregator mints it before any producer runs, and it is hashed into digests. A receipt must be produced by the step it attests, or be named for what it actually checks. Observe the act separately (CB103 coverage).
- **Trace the dispatch before judging a witness (RA-215 F1):** the step-61 decision rejected the rectangular hard-rail fixture as a curved-disk rim witness without tracing it. Under an A3 plan, the uniform and annulus producers decline any region touching a rail, so curved-disk produces it. A Review that cannot name a witness must find one or stop. It must not hand test design to a CB.
- **A producer domain that is not DESIGN's region (P13 Vase, RA-213):** A4 producers built per source-topology region, so a cone, which always splits its region by traces into several A2b discs, looked like a singularity-capability gap. A2b already certified the discs. Before extending a producer, compare its domain with the stage table's unit and check whether the upstream plan it should consume is actually read; on every produced fixture it was not.

- **A per-query check that moves down a level (P13-k):** lending the label support removed the per-quad chart graph, but the same O(F) source binding check then ran once per chart face and once per label query. Re-profile after each cost fix, and remove the check at every call depth: check once per stage, then lend structures that carry their build-time check (RA-201).
- **A whole-collection rescan per element (P13-m):** a check about one node, vertex or transaction walked the whole halfedge list, edge map or active network. The cost hid outside the stage being fixed, and it was memory-bound, so it hit one platform hardest. Profile the whole run, index by the filter key, and check a transaction by its net change (Amendment 34, RA-202).

- **A key built from one consumer's view of a shared object (P13-j):** the completed-patch corner key was the arrangement node seen through the halfedge leaving it in one cell, so the cells around one node published different keys. A validator caught it only where three cracks met; 23,221 other split nodes passed, and a completion test passed on a two-component assembly. Key a shared object by its own identity, assert the assembled topology, and count how widespread a defect is before studying its first witness (RA-200).

- **A selection stage that selects nothing (P13-i):** two conservative predicates (same-face coverage and exact degrees at every junction) made every optional flowline unremovable, so the unsimplified candidate network reached the arrangement as slivers and lenses. Count a selector's accepted removals before routing a sliver to the tracer or the arrangement (RA-198). Then check which relation its guards can see: Vase's lenses are traces that meet nothing, which neither a node-degree rule nor a distance bound can express, because no trace ends on another (RA-199).
- **An index contract between producer and consumer, broken silently (P13-h):** the descriptor started each patch boundary at its smallest halfedge id, while the closed-form grid read corners by index from position 0. 1×1 cells, which no rotation can change, never failed; that was the tell. When a rotation-invariant subclass is always clean, check the ordering contract before the geometry (RA-197).
- **A validator trusted as the oracle can be blind to the defect (P13-g):** the completion embedding check projected each quad onto its own plane, so it accepted folds, and the stage reported only its first failure in semantic order. Check a validator against the source (here the source normal) and count every failure of the pass before naming the owner (RA-196).
- **Immutable authority reconstructed inside a hot validation loop (P13-f):** source-chart graph constructors perform source-wide checks and topological construction; calling them per vertex or per completed patch transforms a shared proof into O((P+V)F) repeated work. Borrow one source-bound immutable graph at an explicit lifetime boundary without weakening authority predicates; do not mistake a 600-second timeout for a completed terminal failure. The binding proof is that the graph borrows exactly the callee's inputs (Amendment 27), not a per-use `matches_source_faces`. Profile a cost defect, and remove it in a probe, before writing the plan (RA-195).

- **Rejection location is not producer identity:** side repair rejected an arrangement that had already failed. Read the producer's own published audit first (`SurfaceArrangementDiagnostics::incidenceFailure`), then probe incidence at each boundary before attributing or repairing (RA-193, 2026-11-01).
- **Identity keys that carry a witness's chart split shared entities:** a wedge, fan or edge key must use the canonical `SourceEntityIdentity`, not `ResolvedSourceEntity`'s per-face chart; and an angle measured from a canonical edge direction is counter-clockwise on only one of its two faces (RA-193).

- **Simple-edge identity loses a slit:** a vertex-indexed face array cannot hold two edges with the same endpoints, so an interior barrier edge with two interior tips stays glued. That is the only configuration that does (RA-192.1 D2). A1 applies it in the cycle basis instead of building a new incidence product, because transport adjacency is already keyed by source edge (RA-191, RA-192).

- **Exact local arithmetic versus unbounded carried rational width:** a finite bit cap cannot bound growth with face count. Amendment 24 rounds each interior exit inside the one producer that every consumer replays, so the trace is its published geometry (RA-190). A representation change made outside that producer would split one trace into two geometries.

- **Platform split:** a result that differs between builds is a determinism defect. Compute the exact answer before classifying, and change per-file flags only through the build system (RA-176, RA-179).
  - A count seen on one platform is not a prediction for another: RA-202.9 pinned macOS's 67,829 and Linux gave 67,784 (O30). A cross-platform registration pins typed verdicts, topological counts and values the executing platform has already produced. Anything else is recorded, not pinned.
  - To localize a split, print bit-exact input digests and per-stage hashes on both platforms rather than reasoning from one (RA-203).
  - **Noise carriers.** A stage hash that includes exact double bits, or an upstream digest, changes with any last-bit noise. The atlas, network, cut graph, topology plan, conformity baseline and tracing hashes are noise carriers. Localize at the first differing stage that is **not** one (RA-204.2).
  - **Order-invariant geometric predicates.** A validity test of a polygon must not depend on which corner starts its cycle or which way it runs. Use the whole-polygon (Newell) vector area and project **orthogonally onto that plane**, not along the nearest coordinate axis, which can be 55° off and hides strongly twisted crossings (RA-209). Give the producer and the validator one shared definition, implemented independently. Fixing the orientation of correct output on its first triangle reversed 7,630 Vase quads (RA-208).
  - **Contact predicates in length units.** An orientation determinant is twice an area; compare a distance (`orient2/|ab|`) with a length tolerance. Fan a concave polygon from its reflex corner. A producer must reject the contacts its validator reports, at the validator's own tolerance (RA-210).
  - **Representation is not a breakpoint (including D7 A3).** A3’s old `m * sum(lambda_i^2)` count and `m*j/n` breakpoint index also incorrectly treated the arbitrary support-piece partition as a physical measure; its exact additive metric is frozen by [RA-216](M6_Consolidated_Record_Vol_D2_CP3_P13_from_20261104.md#architecture_m6_cp3_defn_p13_a3_partition_stable_metric_adversarial_review_20261108md "moved: Architecture_M6_CP3_DEFN_P13_A3_Partition_Stable_Metric_Adversarial_Review_20261108.md") as a run-wise integer count, and placement moves to D7b. A degree-2 node that both incident cells cross on one plain side (a trace crossing a source edge, an arc joint) is an artifact of the per-face embedding; it must not raise a side count, flip parity or become a quad corner (DESIGN §6.2, §7.4). A reflex corner needs completion support; `PureQuadPatch::turns` is not read by any backend (RA-211, RA-212).
  - **Know which producer a fixture runs.** Four census fixtures come from the phase front, which records A3 consumption only on hard rails (RA-213 D4, RA-214 D9), while Vase alone runs the legacy tracing fallback. A fixture that never enters a path cannot falsify a change to it. Before tuning a layout's cells, compare its density with the metric target and count its seeds by provenance (RA-212).
  - **Name the arithmetic that decides an exact contract.** Before freezing an "exact" rule, write the predicate that decides each output and check that the kernel has it at that size. Rounding a sum of k square roots needs 2^k radical monomials in the only multi-radical code; one root (integer square root) is cheap. A defined exact integer function satisfies an objective with permitted deviation. Also check that a consumer exists before specifying a representation change (RA-216).
  - **One decomposition, no new failure mode.** A function that must agree with an earlier one reads the same decomposition, not a re-derived one, and a rounding that can erase a part is replaced by one that cannot (ceiling weights) rather than by a fail-closed rule that turns an unconsumed edge case into a build failure. Before switching a representation, find every accepted test that asserts the old encoding (RA-217: selector:392).
  - **Single writer.** A downstream pass that "fixes" an earlier stage's product is a second writer (DESIGN §3, item 4, M2). Count how often it fires on production before repairing it: with a correct predicate it flipped 0 quads, so it is removed, not repaired (RA-209).
  - **Both sides of a barrier.** A seed or sample named by a source entity that a hard barrier splits goes on each typed fan, never on a row-chosen one and never as a new failure mode. That is the hard-rail rule, and DESIGN §10.1 canonical identity (RA-207).
  - **Wall-clock guards.** A gated time bound is a cross-platform measurement. Set it from the Linux runner's ratio for that code, or better, count the invariant the test claims (`source_binding_check_count`), and keep time only as a coarse backstop (RA-206.6).
  - **Representation order.** Cross-library agreement is not determinism. DESIGN §10.1 also requires source-face row, output row and scheduling permutations to preserve the semantic output. A tie-break on row-derived ids (CB90) fixes the first and not the second. Run a row permutation before calling an order-dependent result deterministic (RA-205).
  - **Unspecified order.** Different standard libraries break `std::sort`, heap and hash-table ties differently. Run the census once under libc++ `-D_LIBCPP_DEBUG_RANDOMIZE_UNSPECIFIED_STABILITY`: if outcomes vary between processes, the split is an order defect, and a total comparator fixes it (O30a, RA-204.4).
- **Representation-row seed collision:** selecting the first incident face row as source-vertex seed position, then deduplicating after earlier rail seeds by face-local barycentrics, suppresses different optional requests depending on face order. Resolve existing typed source-fan identity and canonical source chart instead; require row permutations and a split/coalesce barrier falsifier (O32a Review 2026-11-05). Do not equate lost optional seed annotation with missing producer-owned rail interval.
- **Masked or unconsumed stimulus:** a stronger earlier guard, or a mutation of data that the checked path never reads, makes a negative vacuous; confirm that the intended guard actually fires (RA-169, RA-178, TB35-REV).
- **Oracle copies the producer:** an oracle that re-implements the producer's formula proves nothing; derive it independently (RA-168).
- **Vacuous witness:** RA-146/148 and CB31-REV (corrected statically in CB32-REV): equal-gauge row permutations cannot distinguish stale mapping; unpaired front records do not reach reciprocal periodic action guards. A path that is never reached, or a relation whose operands share an object (RA-124), cannot independently prove its advertised contract. Require separately sourced positive/negative oracles and actual prerequisite reachability.
- **Conflated authority:** row number, gauge, semantic ID, terminal incidence, certificate provenance or a per-node representative face (RA-72.4, RA-123) used as interchangeable identities. Find the unique writer and exact typed correspondence.
- **Premature credit:** compile-green, diagnostic data or focused PASS/RED presented as cumulative acceptance. Separate candidate evidence from reviewed promotion.
- **Predicted cause substituted for measured cause:** inspect the **actual first rejecting predicate** and discriminate its subconditions before proposing a repair.
- **Incomplete case analysis:** a contract excludes a case from one branch without assigning it to another. When a specification says "X cannot enter branch B", ask which branch admits X.
- **Incomplete execution mistaken for expected RED (RA-138):** one test-start marker without a terminal GoogleTest failure is not evidence of a completed negative outcome. Preserve the log but fail the harness.
- **Documentation drift:** a second “CURRENT” section appended rather than revising the existing one. Move incident history into its owning tracker, changelog, report or closure record.

Reusable rules and examples belong in [LESSONS.md](LESSONS.md), not here.

## 9. Code entry points

| Subject | Source |
|---|---|
| Pipeline and product wiring | [`src/pipeline/RemeshPipeline.cpp`](../../src/pipeline/RemeshPipeline.cpp) |

| Geometry/network and pipeline tests | [`FieldAlignedCurveNetworkTests.cpp`](../../tests/FieldAlignedCurveNetworkTests.cpp), [`SurfaceCellsPhase10Tests.cpp`](../../tests/SurfaceCellsPhase10Tests.cpp), [`SurfaceCellPipelinePhase20Tests.cpp`](../../tests/SurfaceCellPipelinePhase20Tests.cpp) |
| Field-aligned network and SurfaceCells diagnostics display (GUI sections 6–7) | [`SourceRegionDisplay.cpp`](../../src/visualization/SourceRegionDisplay.cpp), [`FieldAlignedNetworkPolylines.cpp`](../../src/visualization/FieldAlignedNetworkPolylines.cpp), [`src/gui/Main.cpp`](../../src/gui/Main.cpp) |
| Field streamlines (GUI section 8) | [`FieldStreamlines.cpp`](../../src/visualization/FieldStreamlines.cpp), [`Streamlines.cpp`](../../src/geometry/Streamlines.cpp) |

| Review helper conventions | [`.agents/Directional/tools/README.md`](tools/README.md) |

## 10. Outlook and upkeep

**Path forward:** finish M6-CP3 direct-production acceptance → **M7** verifier-owned disposition and strictly labeled degradation → **M8** module boundaries, scale/quality/resource calibration, packaging and production-ready D0 matrix. Future schedules and selector counts remain **unfrozen**; [ROADMAP.md](ROADMAP.md) owns future checkpoint descriptions.

### DURABLE — DO NOT DELETE, AND UPDATE AT EVERY REVIEW TURN

This landing page must remain easy to scan. Per [REVIEW_TURN_POLICY.md §3.1](REVIEW_TURN_POLICY.md#31-orientationmd--update-before-closing-without-exception), every Review updates the **currency line**, **§3** accepted state/selector/accounting, **§4** when witnesses change, **§7** prioritized open problems (remove resolved/stale claims), and **§8** for genuinely new patterns. Review also performs the required consolidation under [CLEAN_UP_POLICY.md](CLEAN_UP_POLICY.md).

**Never append** per-turn headers, repeated status copies, artifact IDs, detailed diagnostics, regression histories, full test ledgers, or frozen definitions. Update summaries **in place**, linking to the correct owner document. Only substantive changes in the project, fixture matrix or document navigation merit more material here.

The BunnyRandom inventory contains exactly two distinct offending source edges; their parameter values are pairwise different on each edge. TB125 is Test + Benchmark only, not a correction, selector promotion, or independent Review.

## Background — exact immutable authority

All four result ZIPs were independently verified from their SHA256SUMS files.

## Assessment — required checks

| Requirement | Evidence |
|---|---|
| Full gate | 950 planned/executed/passed, 0 RED, 0 missing/skipped/duplicate/abnormal, 1,031 membership, `postflight=true`, `exit=0` |
| Inherited ledger | Rows 1–949 identical to TB124 in **every substantive field**; only log SHA changes where raw diagnostic output varies |
| New inventory test | Row 950 `MandatoryEdgeTerminalInventory.OrdersCutsExactlyAndClassesEqualParameters` PASS, one execution, zero skips |
| Gate inventory | Zero `mandatoryEdgeTerminalInventory` lines in 950 raw logs |
| Raw gate comparison | After normalizing only GoogleTest elapsed times, six rows differ: 164, 173, 175, 176, 195, 477. Differences are only ordering/line joining of `M3_CP4AB_FRAGMENT_DIAG`; exact per-row diagnostic record multisets match. Removing those prints leaves **949/949** inherited logs equal. No semantic output difference is hidden. |
| Census | Cylinder PASS; BunnyRandom RED; Vase RED; zero abnormal, missing, skipped and duplicate; `postflight=true`, `exit=0` |
| Census receipts and terminals | All three substantive ledgers equal TB122 except raw-log SHA. After removing the new inventory lines, and normalizing GoogleTest elapsed times and the process-dependent `GetParam()` object bytes, **3/3** complete raw logs equal TB122; required `FieldTransportAtlas.partition edgePorts`, `FieldAlignedCurveNetwork.vertexOutflowFollow`, `switch-run`, `platform-digest`, and typed terminal receipts are unchanged. |
| Runtime boundary | Gate and census `compile=false`, `relink=false`, `configure=false`, `benchmark=false`; neither executed an unapproved build or modified the immutable package |

**BunnyRandom observed inventory** (`SurfaceCutGraph.mandatoryEdgeTerminalInventory`):

| Source edge | Exact sort order (trace, node, printed t) | Cuts | Distinct parameters | Equal parameters share node |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| `(241,251)` | `(107,149,0.29728465697386852)`, `(38,178,0.6954898086630259)` | 2 | 2 | true |
| `(251,278)` | `(92,184,0.24658061360047423)`, `(41,162,0.26252359514325541)` | 2 | 2 | true |

The four printed inventory lines contain **two distinct edges**, each observed twice with identical details. Unique inventoried edges = **2**; unique edges with `distinctParameters < cuts` = **0**. These are **observations only** under RA-243 (P4/P5), not additional MATCH gates. The `equalParametersShareNode=true` value is vacuous for each edge because it has no equal-parameter pair. The decimal `t` values are diagnostic display values; equality and order are derived by the program's exact rational comparisons, not by these decimal strings. Do **not** infer that duplicates are generally impossible or that CB144 is already proven valid.

**Census rejection:** BunnyRandom remains `MandatoryEdgeTerminalOrderUnresolved` at source edge `(241,251)` and fails closed; Vase retains its previous non-mandatory-edge failure; Cylinder remains green. The inventory precedes the existing rejection and does not change output connectivity. No accepted-green loss, new first-failure locus, unexpected control result, or stable regression is established. C3 remains an open non-stable P13-e candidate with newly bounded cause data; O42/O43 remain open. O44/C1 are closed. Stable events/categories/recurrences remain **66/17/49**, debt **1**, deferred **81**, CP2 accepted **491/491**. CP3 and selector497 remain unpromoted; `chainRimSchedules=false`; D11=A, D12=A, D10 deferred, D6 pending.

## Recommendation — strict turn boundary

TB125 is **MATCH** under RA-243 §4. The next **separate Review-only** turn is `M6-CP3-DEFN-P13-EDGE-TERMINAL-EQUIVALENCE-DECISION`. It must use the exact inventory to decide the source owner and safe topology correction before authorizing CB144. The present turn does **not** select, code, compile, test or run the CB144 successor; it does not revise fixtures, selector membership, existing assertions, baseline regressions or PR metadata.

### Regression assessment — candidate root cause and limits

The exception is consistent with an invalid test identity, not yet with a demonstrated production split failure. In `tests/FieldAlignedCurveNetworkTests.cpp:18588–18598`, both typed ID factories use `idCount=16U`. At line 18650, the test constructs `cut(38,13,...)`; its `trace(38)` calls `TraceId::from_index(38,16).value()`. `SemanticId::from_index` (`include/directional/authority/AuthorityIds.h:182–195`) returns `IndexOutOfRange` for any index >= extent, and `DomainResult::value()` (lines 84–85) uses `std::get<T>` on that error variant. The resulting exception matches the observed log. The later `trace(38)` expected values also exceed the declared extent. This source-derived explanation is a candidate, not a runtime stack trace; the guard does not establish that the N=2 chain or its subsequent exact/equal/endpoint/shared-node controls pass. An independent Review must establish the first failing site, preserve all assertions, and decide any test-only correction. Do not fix the test or relax a validator in TB126.

No previously accepted green row was lost. Row 951 was newly introduced, and BunnyRandom was already RED; therefore neither observation establishes a new stable regression ID or historical-count change. C3 remains open pending independent Review; the new rotation-system terminal is a separate unclassified non-stable P13-e candidate. O42/O43 open; O44/C1 closed. Stable 66 events / 17 categories / 49 recurrences, debt 1, deferred 81; CP2 accepted 491/491; CP3/selector497 unpromoted; `chainRimSchedules=false`; D11=A, D12=A, D10 deferred, D6 pending.
